- **pv-compiler**: Read-only documentation compiler for the final report. Proves nothing, re-adjudicates nothing. ([ziwaa-se/kappa](https://github.com/ziwaa-se/kappa/blob/HEAD/template/.claude/agents/pv-compiler.md))
- **pv-drafter**: Drafts faithful Lean 4 statements (with sorry) for one numbered result, splitting the paper's proof into step obligations tied to exact source lines, and declares external inputs as visible Prop parameters. Also drafts shared definitions (Defs). Use before the statement gate; never proves. ([ziwaa-se/kappa](https://github.com/ziwaa-se/kappa/blob/HEAD/template/.claude/agents/pv-drafter.md))
- **pv-factcheck**: Independent fact-checker for the final author-facing report. Verifies every claim (verdict classes, conditions, file:line references, classifications, Lean-evidence claims, suggested fixes) against the paper, the audits and the records, and flags invented suggestions and wrong severities. Read-only except its own report. ([ziwaa-se/kappa](https://github.com/ziwaa-se/kappa/blob/HEAD/template/.claude/agents/pv-factcheck.md))
- **pv-gate**: Independent statement-fidelity auditor. Compares a drafted Lean statement, its step obligations and external-input parameters against the exact LaTeX source before any proof work. Returns PASS, PASS_WITH_AMENDMENTS or FAIL with rulings. Never edits the draft. ([ziwaa-se/kappa](https://github.com/ziwaa-se/kappa/blob/HEAD/template/.claude/agents/pv-gate.md))
- **pv-investigator**: Pharmacovigilance signal investigation agent. Runs multi-step drug safety analyses using NexVigilant Station MCP tools. Use for comprehensive signal detection, causality assessment, and regulatory intelligence. ([nexvigilant/nexvigilant-plugin](https://github.com/nexvigilant/nexvigilant-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/pv-investigator.md))
- **pv-issue-checker**: Bounded adjudication of a suspected problem in the paper (an unsupported step, a suspicious claim, a cited tool that may not apply). Classifies it (error / argument gap / missing assumption / exposition gap / domain / fair implicit step / formalization-side), states whether the result's conclusion is threatened, and builds a Lean counterexample or obstruction when possible. Never patches the proof. ([ziwaa-se/kappa](https://github.com/ziwaa-se/kappa/blob/HEAD/template/.claude/agents/pv-issue-checker.md))
- **pv-prover**: Proves the step obligations of one gated statement in Lean 4 strictly along the paper's own proof, within a fixed attempt budget; at a step the paper does not justify it stops, keeps that step as an explicit gap hypothesis in a separate ModGaps file, and reports. Also writes predecessor reconnections (&lt;ID&gt;Closed_r&lt;k&gt;.lean). Never changes a gated statement, never finds an alternative proof. ([ziwaa-se/kappa](https://github.com/ziwaa-se/kappa/blob/HEAD/template/.claude/agents/pv-prover.md))
- **PVA Eternal Custodian**: Long-term guardian for PVAGR/pva-bazaar-app and pvabazaar.org. Maintains production uptime, enforces security hygiene, operates the OpenClaw gateway, and guides maintainers with disciplined runbooks and predictable releases. ([PVAGR/pva-bazaar-app](https://github.com/PVAGR/pva-bazaar-app/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/my-agent.agent.md) · ★1)
- **PVA Magnum Opus**: The definitive agent for PVAGR/pva-bazaar-app and pvabazaar.org. Guardian of the Magnum Opus archive, operator of the OpenClaw gateway, maintainer of production uptime and security hygiene. Talks with the creator, operates the website, and ensures continuity across all layers of the stack. ([PVAGR/pva-bazaar-app](https://github.com/PVAGR/pva-bazaar-app/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/pva-magnum-opus.agent.md) · ★1)
- **pvbt-strategy-reviewer**: Use this agent when Go code implementing a pvbt strategy has been written or modified. It reviews recently changed strategy code for correctness, pvbt idioms, and quant red flags such as survivorship bias and lookahead bias. Invoke after strategy edits. Examples: - user: "Write a momentum rotation strategy across SPY, EFA, EEM." assistant: [writes strategy] Since pvbt strategy code was written, use the pvbt-strategy-reviewer agent to review it. assistant: "Let me use the pvbt-strategy-reviewer agent to check this against pvbt best practices." - user: "I refactored the signal calculation in my… ([penny-vault/pvbt-strategy-author](https://github.com/penny-vault/pvbt-strategy-author/blob/HEAD/agents/pvbt-strategy-reviewer.md))
- **pvd-bash-verifier**: Read-only verifier in the Prover–Verifier–Decoder protocol for bash execution. Invoked by the prover (main Claude Code session) before each state-mutating bash command. Evaluates the structured proposal against a security checklist and returns a JSON verdict (ACCEPT / CHALLENGE / ABSTAIN). Capability-asymmetric by design: this subagent runs on Haiku while the prover runs on Sonnet — the asymmetry is the source of the selection signal per the paper. ([jsedoc/prover-verifier-deliberation](https://github.com/jsedoc/prover-verifier-deliberation/blob/HEAD/skills/prover-verifier-bash/agents/pvd-bash-verifier.md) · ★3)
- **pvd-opus-escalator**: Senior reviewer for Prover–Verifier Deliberation. Invoked only when the Sonnet prover and Haiku verifier have spent 12 rounds without reaching Accept + No Change (ANC), or after 3 consecutive Rejects. Either produces a fresh redesign that addresses every outstanding challenge, or recommends a re-scope of the task. Has write access — its diff is final for this PVD loop (re-verifying it would recurse indefinitely). ([jsedoc/prover-verifier-deliberation](https://github.com/jsedoc/prover-verifier-deliberation/blob/HEAD/skills/prover-verifier-code/agents/pvd-opus-escalator.md) · ★3)
- **pvd-verifier**: Read-only verifier in the Prover–Verifier Deliberation protocol. Invoked by the prover (main Claude Code session) after each code change. Inspects the current code against the prover's sub-claims, picks the most suspicious one, and returns a structured verdict (Accept / Reject / Challenge) with style notes and suggested unit tests. Capability-asymmetric on purpose: this subagent is Haiku, the prover is Sonnet — the asymmetry is the source of the selection signal per the paper. ([jsedoc/prover-verifier-deliberation](https://github.com/jsedoc/prover-verifier-deliberation/blob/HEAD/skills/prover-verifier-code/agents/pvd-verifier.md) · ★3)
- **pve-lab**: Routine operator for the CoreOS Platform Lab Proxmox host (Mode A). Use for inventory, status, and lifecycle work on project guests in the `corelab` pool — clone from the FCOS template, configure, start/stop, snapshot/rollback, backup/restore on the project storage, delete — and for writing FCOS Ignition snippets through the restricted snippet writer. It drafts cleanup manifests and host actions for the owner but never executes privileged host work. Do NOT use for host networking, storage definitions, users/tokens/ACLs, package upgrades, reboots, or anything outside the pool; those are owner-… ([w7-mgfcode/coreos-platform-lab](https://github.com/w7-mgfcode/coreos-platform-lab/blob/HEAD/lab-agent/proposed/agents/pve-lab.md) · ★2)

- **pvr-verifier**: Independent verifier for PHP vulnerability patches. Use after patching to confirm, without relying on the patcher's claims, that every host and site is fixed. Read-only. ([luivc1/php-vuln-responder](https://github.com/luivc1/php-vuln-responder/blob/HEAD/agents/pvr-verifier.md))
- **pvs-arquiteto**: Use quando a tarefa for grande ou ambígua e precisar de decisão de design/arquitetura ANTES de codar — KISS, decide o mínimo suficiente, sem over-engineering. - **pvt-simulation**: Creates and runs NeqSim PVT laboratory simulations: constant mass expansion (CME/CCE), CVD, differential liberation, separator and swelling tests, saturation pressure/temperature, GOR and viscosity. - **pw-explorer**: Live-app explorer. - **pw-healer**: Self-healing specialist. - **pw-orchestrator**: Orchestrates Playwright test agents based on source file changes. Determines which endpoints need testing and delegates to planner, generator, and healer agents. - **pw-page-mapper**: Compresses one Playwright accessibility snapshot into a short structured map — forms, navigation, controls, messages and counts — carrying every element ref verbatim. - **pw-planner**: Playwright test planner. - **pw-researcher**: Answer a scoped question with evidence (Mode A — file:line paths, what-was-searched on misses) or ground a scope of human-dropped inputs against real state into a ground-truth pack + seed (Mode B). - **pw-review-scanner**: Scans changed test files for the mechanically detectable review findings and returns them as file:line hits mapped to checklist categories. - **pw-reviewer**: Quality gate reviewer.
